Local search details with power p.

Algorithm local_search(a, budget, p0, p_end, step):
  n=len(a); a=positive normalize sum1.
  best=a copy; best_score=fast_score(a)
  p=p0; step=1.0; no_improve=0; stagnation_steps...
  while time<limit:
    if no_improve > threshold: perturb best with mild noise or switch p/step.
    Compute c,C, score.
    If C<=0 break.
    Compute weights w=(c/C)^p normalized.
    Compute H.
    delta=H - np.dot(a,H) (centered)
    range = max(abs(delta.min()), abs(delta.max()))? Use range = delta.max - delta.min.
    if range < 1e-15: random small perturbation; p maybe adjust; continue.
    lambda = step / range
    # maybe cap min exponent to avoid underflow: max_factor=3? If step<=2, safe.
    cand = a * np.exp(-lambda * delta)
    cand = np.clip(cand,0,None)
    if cand.sum==0 or not finite: step*=0.5; continue
    cand/=cand.sum()
    cand_score=fast_score(cand) # Could reuse c? Need new c. We'll compute.
    if cand_score < best_score - tol:
       # line search maybe try larger step to accelerate
       # evaluate cand2 = a*exp(-2*lambda*delta) maybe? But can just step*=1.1
       a=cand; best=cand; best_score=score; no_improve=0; step=min(3,step*1.05)
    else:
       # try smaller step half
       cand2 maybe half. If improves, use; else step*=0.8; no_improve+=1
    # periodically adjust p: start p=1, increase to 8? Need choose.
    p = min(p_end, p*(1+decay))? maybe oscillate.

But if we evaluate cand_score with fast_score, it recomputes c; we need c for current for weights. One extra FFT per iter. Could be okay. We can avoid line search by not evaluating cand? Need know improvement. We'll evaluate.

As p increases, weights emphasize large c. But if p too high and symmetric, may plateau. We can perform p adaptation: start p=1 (global), gradually increase to 20 (peak). Or alternate. Use p based on current score? If score near target, use high p. Let's set p_start=2, p_end=50, linearly increase over local search. Also if stagnation, decrease p to explore.

Step schedule: start step=0.5, can grow to 2.0; if no improvement reduce. Could use fixed step=0.7 always? Let's design robust:

```
def _power_weights(c, C, p):
    x = c / C
    x = np.clip(x, 0.0, 1.0)
    if p == 0:
       w = np.full(L, 1.0)
    else:
       # use log to avoid underflow? p up to 50, x^p may underflow but ok.
       w = np.power(x, p)
    s = w.sum()
    if s <= 0 or not finite: w = c if c.sum>0 else ones; s=c.sum()
    return w / s
```

```
def run_local_search(a, seed, deadline, p_start=1.0, p_end=30.0, step0=0.7, top=0.0, noise_sigma=0.02):
   n=a.size; N=...
   a=normalize...
   best=a.copy(); best_score=fast_score...
   rng=default_rng(seed)
   p=p_start; step=step0; no_improve=0; total=0
   while time.monotonic()<deadline:
       c, C = convolution... (score = 2*n*C)
       if not finite(score): break
       if score < best_score: best... (should be current)
       if best_score <= target? maybe continue or break if very low.
       weights...
       H=...
       center=np.dot(a,H); delta=H-center
       span=delta.max()-delta.min()
```

Helper functions:
1. `safe_array(v)`? maybe.
2. `next_fft_len(n)`:
```
L=2*n-1
N=1
while N<L: N*=2
return N
```
3. `normalize_sequence(a, floor=0.0)`:
```
a=np.asarray(a,dtype=np.float64).ravel()
if a.size==0: return np.array([1.0])
a=np.where(np.isfinite(a),a,0.0)
a=np.clip(a,0.0,1000.0) # maybe but our normalized <1000
s=a.sum()
if not finite or s<=0: a=np.ones_like(a); s=a.sum()
a=a/s
if floor>0: a=np.maximum(a, floor); a/=a.sum()
return a
```
4. `convolve_fft(a, N=None)`:
```
n=a.size; if N None: N=next_fft_len(n)
fa=np.fft.rfft(a,N)
c=np.fft.irfft(fa*fa,N)
c=c[:2*n-1]
c=np.maximum(c,0.0)
return c
```
5. `fast_sequence_score(a, return_c=False)`:
```
a=np.asarray(a,float); n=a.size
s=a.sum(); if s<=0: return inf, None
a=a/s
c=convolve_fft(a)
C=c.max()
score=2.0*n*C
if return_c: return score,c
return score
```
Note if C extremely small, score maybe 0 (all zero invalid but we normalize). Good.

6. `gradient_H(a, c, C, p, N=None)`:
```

   # use logs for stability? p up to 100; x^p underflows but okay. Use np.power.
   w=np.power(x,p)

fa=np.fft.rfft(a,N)

idx=(N - np.arange(n)) % N
H=h[idx]
H=np.maximum(H,0) # numerical negative? Maybe cannot negative; but roundoff. If H all zero? okay.

```

```
def local_search(a, deadline, rng, target=1.5, max_iter=None):
    a=normalize_sequence(a, floor=1e-12)
    n=a.size; N=next_fft_len(n)
    best=a.copy()
    # Get c for current to compute best_score efficiently
    c=convolve_fft(a,N); best_score=2*n*c.max()
    p=2.0; step=0.7; no_improve=0; it=0; total=5
    while time.monotonic() < deadline:
        it+=1
        # if it %? no prints
        c=convolve_fft(a,N)
        C=c.max()
        score=2*n*C
        if score < best_score:
            best_score=score; best=a.copy(); no_improve=0
        # p dynamic
        p = max(2.0, min(80.0, (2.3 - best_score)*25.0)) # score 2 ->2.25? 2.3-2=.3*25=7.5; maybe too high. Let's adjust: (2.0-best_score)*20+2: score2->2, 1.5->12. ok.
        if best_score > 2.0: p=2.0 # for symmetric, low p full
        else: p = min(80.0, max(1.0, (2.0 - best_score)*25.0 + 1.0))
        weights H = gradient_H(a,c,C,p,N)
        # center
        center = np.dot(a, H)
        delta = H - center
        span = delta.max() - delta.min()
        if span <= 1e-14:
            # random perturb
            cand = random_perturb(a, 0.05, rng)
            sc=fast_score_from_c? (need conv)
        else:
            cand, ok = apply_log_update(a, delta, step)
            if not ok: cand = random_perturb...
            sc = fast_sequence_score(cand)
            if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < best_score - 1e-12:
                a=cand; best=a.copy(); best_score=sc; no_improve=0; step=min(2.0, step*1.02)
            else:
                # try half
                cand2, ok2 = apply_log_update(a, delta, step*0.5)
                if ok2:
                   sc2=fast_sequence_score(cand2)
                   if sc2 < best_score -1e-12:
                       a=cand2; best=...; best_score=sc2; no_improve=0; step=max(0.05,step*0.9)
                if not improved:
                   no_improve +=1
                   step=max(0.02, step*0.8)

                      # random perturb with sigma 0.02..0.1

                      else: a=best.copy() # reset

            # Could still polish; maybe continue until deadline with high p small step
            step = min(step, 0.3)
        if it % 100 == 0:
            print(...)
    return best, best_score
```

```
def optimize_fourier_coords(n, M, seed, iters=200):
    rng=default_rng(seed)
    best_coeffs = np.zeros(2*M)
    best_a=make_fourier_sequence(best_coeffs,n,M); best_score=fast_score(best_a)
    # random restarts maybe 3
    for restart in range(n_starts):
       coeffs = rng.normal(0, 0.4, 2*M)
       # set coeff0=0.5 mode1 sin? maybe add
       for step in [0.1,0.05,0.02,0.005]:
          for it in range(iters):
             improved=False
             for d in range(2*M):
                base=coeffs[d]
                # scan offsets [-3, -2.5, ... , 3] * step
                best_val=base; best_sc=best_score
                for mult in np.linspace(-4,4,17):
                    cand=coeffs.copy(); cand[d]=np.clip(best_val + mult*step, -1.0,1.0)
                    sc=fast_score(make_fourier(cand,n,M))
                    if sc<best_sc: best_sc=sc; best_val=cand[d]
                if best_val != base:
                   coeffs[d]=best_val; best_score=best_sc; best_coeffs=coeffs; improved=True
             if not improved: break
       if best_score < global: global...
    return best_coeffs
```

```
def fourier_search(n,M,maxiter,seed, limit_deadline=None):
   sin_tab,cos_tab = basis_for_fourier(n,M)
   N=next_fft_len(n)
   res_list=[]
   rng=default_rng(seed)
   starts=...
   for s in range(starts):
      x0 = rng.uniform(-0.5,0.5, 2*M)
      # create variants:
      variants=[]
      x0[0]=0.5; variants.append(x0.copy())
      x0[0]=-0.5; ...
      # maybe known initial: sin mode1 0.5, sin mode2 0.3
   for x0 in variants:
      if time>limit: break
      try:
        res=minimize(fourier_objective, x0, args=(sin_tab,cos_tab,N), method='Nelder-Mead', options={'maxiter':maxiter,'xatol':1e-5,'fatol':1e-5})
        if res.fun finite:
           a=make_fourier_basis(res.x, sin_tab, cos_tab)
           sc=fast_sequence_score(a,N)
           res_list.append((sc,a))
      except Exception:
         pass
# if not list, random scan fallback
return res_list
```

```
if a.size==0: return np.ones(1)

a=np.maximum(a,0.0)

if not np.isfinite(s) or s<=1e-300:
    a=np.ones_like(a); s=a.sum()
a=a/s
if floor>0:
    a=np.maximum(a, float(floor))
    s=a.sum()
    if np.isfinite(s) and s>0: a=a/s
return a
```


- `local_search(a, deadline, rng)`:
Let's write full with handling. Need p schedule. Use `it=0`. Inside we can print every 500.
Key: At start, compute c and best_score to avoid duplicate. `a = normalize_positive(a, 1e-12)`. If invalid return.
```
def local_search(a, deadline, rng):
    a=normalize_positive(a,1e-12)
    n=a.size; N=next_fft_len(n)
    best=a.copy()
    c=convolve_fft(a,N)
    best_score=2.0*n*c.max()
    if not finite: return best, best_score
    step=0.7
    no_improve=0
    max_no_improve=300
    it=0
    while time.monotonic() < deadline:
       it+=1
       # exact if time? no
       c=convolve_fft(a,N)
       C=float(c.max())
       score=2.0*n*C
       if np.isfinite(score) and score < best_score:
           best_score=score; best=a.copy(); no_improve=0
       if best_score <= 0: break
       # dynamic p
       if best_score > 2.0 + 1e-6:
           p = 1.0
       else:
           p = 1.0 + max(0.0, 2.0 - best_score) * 25.0
           if p < 1.0: p=1.0
           if p > 80.0: p=80.0
       H=gradient_H(a,c,C,p,N)
       center=float(np.dot(a,H))
       delta=H-center
       span=delta.max()-delta.min()
       improved=False
       if span > 1e-14:
           cand=apply_log_shift(a, delta, step)
           if cand is None:
              cand=random_log_perturb(a,0.05,rng)
           sc=fast_sequence_score(cand,N)
           if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < best_score - 1e-12:
               a=cand; best=a.copy(); best_score=sc; no_improve=0; step=min(2.0, step*1.02); improved=True
           else:
               cand2=apply_log_shift(a, delta, step*0.5)
               if cand2 is not None:
                  sc2=fast_sequence_score(cand2,N)
                  if np.isfinite(sc2) and sc2 < best_score -1e-12:
                      a=cand2; best=a.copy(); best_score=sc2; no_improve=0; step=max(0.05, step*0.9); improved=True
               if not improved:
                  no_improve += 1
                  step=max(0.02, step*0.85)
       else:
          no_improve +=1
          cand=random_log_perturb(a,0.05,rng)
          sc=fast_sequence_score(cand,N)
          if finite and sc<best_score: update
          else: ...
       if no_improve > 80:
           sigma=0.02 + rng.random()*0.08
           cand=random_log_perturb(best, sigma, rng)
           sc=fast_sequence_score(cand,N)
           if finite and sc<best_score:
              a=cand; best=...; no_improve=0; step=0.7
           else:
              a=best.copy()
              no_improve=0
              step=0.7
       if no_improve > max_no_improve:
           break
       if it % 500 == 0:
           print(...)
    return best, best_score
```

```
def fourier_search_candidates(n, M, maxiter, seed, limit_deadline):

       a=make_fourier_basis(x0,...); sc=fast...

    # sort and return unique top
```

Let's consider if our local search might produce sequence with sum 1 but due floor, some entries >1000? No.

Detailed code:
```
import numpy as np
import time
import operator
try:
    from scipy.optimize import minimize
    _HAVE_SCIPY = True
except Exception:
    _HAVE_SCIPY = False

_TARGET = 1.5027

def next_fft_len(n):
    L = 2*int(n)-1
    N = 1
    while N < L:
        N <<= 1
    return int(N)

def normalize_positive(a, floor=0.0):
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).reshape(-1)
    if a.size == 0:
        return np.ones(1, dtype=np.float64)
    a = np.where(np.isfinite(a), a, 0.0)
    a = np.maximum(a, 0.0)
    s = float(a.sum())
    if not np.isfinite(s) or s <= 1e-300:
        a = np.ones_like(a)
        s = float(a.sum())
    a = a / s
    if floor > 0.0:
        a = np.maximum(a, float(floor))
        s = float(a.sum())
        if np.isfinite(s) and s > 0.0:
            a = a / s
    return a

def convolve_fft(a, N=None):
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64)
    n = a.size
    L = 2*n - 1
    if N is None:
        N = next_fft_len(n)
    else:
        N = int(N)
        if N < L:
            N = next_fft_len(n)
    fa = np.fft.rfft(a, N)
    c = np.fft.irfft(fa * fa, N)
    c = c[:L]
    c = np.where(c > 0.0, c, 0.0)
    return c

def fast_score_normalized(a, N=None):
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64)
    n = a.size
    if n == 0:
        return float('inf')
    s = float(a.sum())
    if not np.isfinite(s) or s <= 1e-300:
        return float('inf')
    if abs(s - 1.0) > 1e-9:
        a = a / s
    c = convolve_fft(a, N)
    C = float(c.max()) if c.size else 0.0
    if not np.isfinite(C):
        return float('inf')
    return float(2.0 * n * C)
```
If a contains negative? We assume normalized. But to be safe in candidate, use normalize before. In local, okay. `a = a/s` creates view? okay. If a contains negative due random? no.

`basis_for_fourier`:
```
def basis_for_fourier(n, M):
    n=int(n); M=int(M)
    if n <= 1:
        return np.zeros((M,1)), np.ones((M,1))
    M=max(1,min(M,n//4 if n>4 else 1))
    x = np.arange(n, dtype=np.float64) / float(n-1)
    k = np.arange(1, M+1, dtype=np.float64)[:, None] * 2.0 * np.pi
    angles = k * x[None, :]
    sin_tab = np.sin(angles)
    cos_tab = np.cos(angles)
    return sin_tab, cos_tab
```
If n=4096, n//4=1024, M fine. For n small, M=1.

`make_fourier_basis`:
```
def make_fourier_basis(coeffs, sin_tab, cos_tab):
    M = sin_tab.shape[0]
    n = sin_tab.shape[1]
    coeffs = np.asarray(coeffs, dtype=np.float64).reshape(-1)
    if coeffs.size < 2*M:
       tmp=np.zeros(2*M); tmp[:coeffs.size]=coeffs; coeffs=tmp
    coeffs = coeffs[:2*M]
    coeffs = np.where(np.isfinite(coeffs), coeffs, 0.0)
    coeffs = np.clip(coeffs, -1.0, 1.0)
    f = 1.0 + coeffs[0::2] @ sin_tab + coeffs[1::2] @ cos_tab
    f = np.maximum(f, 0.0)
    f = f + 1e-10
    s = float(f.sum())
    if not np.isfinite(s) or s <= 0.0:
        f = np.ones(n); s=float(f.sum())
    f = f / s
    return f
```
If M=0? no.

`fourier_objective`:
```
def fourier_objective(coeffs, sin_tab, cos_tab, N):
    a = make_fourier_basis(coeffs, sin_tab, cos_tab)
    return fast_score_normalized(a, N)
```

`gradient_H` as above. Ensure c has size L=2n-1. If N not power >=L, recompute? It receives N from local. Use:
```
def gradient_H(a, c, C, p, N=None):
   n=a.size; L=2*n-1
   if C <= 0 or not np.isfinite(C): return np.zeros(n)
   if N is None: N=next_fft_len(n)
   N=int(N)
   x = np.asarray(c, dtype=np.float64) / C
   x = np.clip(x, 0.0, 1.0)
   if p <= 1e-12:
      w=np.ones(L)
   else:
      # If p large, use exp(p*log(x))? x zeros => -inf. np.power okay.
      with np.errstate(over='ignore', under='ignore', invalid='ignore'):
          w=np.power(x, float(p))
      w=np.where(np.isfinite(w), w, 0.0)
   s=float(w.sum())
   if not np.isfinite(s) or s <= 1e-300:
      w=c.copy(); s=float(w.sum())
   if not np.isfinite(s) or s <= 1e-300:
      w=np.ones(L); s=float(w.sum())
   w=w/s
   r=np.zeros(N, dtype=np.float64)
   r[0]=w[0]
   if L > 1:
      r[N-1:N-L:-1] = w[:0:-1]
   fa=np.fft.rfft(a, N)
   fr=np.fft.rfft(r, N)
   h=np.fft.irfft(fa*fr, N)
   idx=(N - np.arange(n)) % N
   H=h[idx]
   H=np.where(H>0.0, H, 0.0)
   if float(H.max()) <= 0.0:
      H=np.ones_like(a)
      s=float(H.sum())
      H=H/s
   return H
```
The slice `N-1:N-L:-1` if N-L == N-1? L=1 skip. If N-L maybe 0; stop 0. Good. For N power might equal L? If L not power, N > L. Good.
`w[:0:-1]` length L-1. For L=1 skip.

Potential issue: The weights w sum normalized to 1. But gradient H scale with sum weights; if p high top w=1, H ~a. If p low w sum1 over many, H ~1/(2n)*? scale ~1/n. Span centering. Step based on span independent. Good.

`apply_log_shift`:
```
def apply_log_shift(a, delta, step):
    delta = np.asarray(delta, dtype=np.float64)
    span = float(delta.max() - delta.min())
    if span <= 1e-15 or not np.isfinite(span):
       return None
    lam = float(step) / span
    if not np.isfinite(lam): return None
    ex = -lam * delta
    ex = np.clip(ex, -8.0, 8.0)
    cand = a * np.exp(ex)
    cand = np.maximum(cand, 1e-12)
    s = float(cand.sum())
    if not np.isfinite(s) or s <= 0.0:
       return None
    cand = cand / s
    return cand
```
If delta center, positive for low H. Good.

`random_perturb`:
```
def random_log_perturb(a, sigma, rng):
    base = np.maximum(a, 1e-12)
    loga = np.log(base)
    loga = loga + rng.normal(0.0, float(sigma), size=a.size)
    loga = np.clip(loga, -60.0, 60.0)
    cand = np.exp(loga)
    cand = np.maximum(cand, 1e-12)
    s = float(cand.sum())
    if not np.isfinite(s) or s <= 0.0:
        return base.copy()
    return cand / s
```
No normalize positive needed.

`random_positive_candidates`:
```
def random_positive_candidates(n, count, seed):
   rng=np.random.default_rng(seed)
   out=[]
   for i in range(count):
      kind=i%3
      if kind==0:
         a=np.exp(rng.normal(0.0,0.35,n))
      elif kind==1:
         a=rng.gamma(2.0, size=n)
      else:
         a=np.ones(n) + 0.6*rng.normal(0.0,1.0,n)
      a=normalize_positive(a, 1e-12)
      out.append(a)
   return out
```

`generate_basic_candidates`:
```
def generate_basic_candidates(n, M, seed):
    n=int(n); M=max(1,min(M, max(1,n//4)))
    sin_tab, cos_tab=basis_for_fourier(n,M)
    N=next_fft_len(n)
    rng=np.random.default_rng(seed)
    out=[]
    out.append(np.ones(n)/n)
    # sine的神奇 etc
    alphas = [0.3,0.5,0.7,0.9,-0.3,-0.5,-0.7]
    for al in alphas:
       coeffs=np.zeros(2*M)
       coeffs[0]=al
       out.append(make_fourier_basis(coeffs, sin_tab, cos_tab))
    # two-mode
    coeffs=np.zeros(2*M); coeffs[0]=0.5
    if M>=2: coeffs[2]=0.3
    out.append(make_fourier_basis(...))
    coeffs[0]=0.5; coeffs[2]=-0.3; out.append
    # random fourier 8
    for i in range(8):
       coeffs=rng.normal(0.0,0.35,size=2*M)
       coeffs[0]+=0.4*(1 if rng.random()<0.5 else -1)
       out.append(...)
    # random positive 3
    for a in random_positive_candidates(n,3,seed+123): out.append(a)
    # score? not here; caller
    return out
```
We may generate many candidates (20) per length; scoring 40 FFT okay. Could be too many? fine.

`fourier_search_candidates`:
```
def fourier_search_candidates(n, M, maxiter, seed, limit_deadline):
    n=int(n); M=max(1,min(M, max(1,n//64)))
    sin_tab, cos_tab = basis_for_fourier(n,M)
    N=next_fft_len(n)
    rng=np.random.default_rng(seed)
    starts=[]
    c=np.zeros(2*M); c[0]=0.5; starts.append(c.copy())
    c[0]=-0.5; starts.append(c.copy())
    c2=np.zeros(2*M); c2[0]=0.5
    if M>=2: c2[2]=0.3
    starts.append(c2.copy())
    c2[2]=-0.3 if M>=2 else 0; starts.append(c2.copy())
    for i in range(4):
       starts.append(rng.normal(0.0,0.4,size=2*M))
    out=[]
    if _HAVE_SCIPY:
       for x0 in starts:
          if time.monotonic() >= limit_deadline: break
          try:
             res = minimize(fourier_objective, x0, args=(sin_tab, cos_tab, N),
                            method='Nelder-Mead',
                            options={'maxiter': int(maxiter), 'xatol':1e-5, 'fatol':1e-5, 'adaptive':False})
             if res.fun is not None and np.isfinite(res.fun):
                a=make_fourier_basis(res.x, sin_tab, cos_tab)
                sc=fast_score_normalized(a,N)
                out.append((sc,a))
          except Exception:
             pass
    # random fallback/add
    count = 30 if not _HAVE_SCIPY else 8
    for i in range(count):
       x0=rng.normal(0.0,0.4,size=2*M)
       x0[0] = 0.4*(1 if rng.random()<0.5 else -1) + 0.1*rng.normal()
       a=make_fourier_basis(x0, sin_tab, cos_tab)
       sc=fast_score_normalized(a,N)
       out.append((sc,a))
    out.sort(key=operator.itemgetter(0))
    # dedup capacity 6
    res=[]
    prev=None
    for sc,a in out[:12]:
       if not np.isfinite(sc): continue
       res.append((sc,a.copy()))
       if len(res)>=6: break
    return res
```
`minimize` options `adaptive` maybe not accepted by Nelder? It is. Could skip to be safe. Just maxiter, xatol, fatol. Good.

Potential: `xatol`: Nelder-Mead accepts `xatol`, `fatol`. yes.

`local_search` code with prints. Need pass `target`? maybe not.
```
def local_search(a, deadline, rng):
    a = normalize_positive(a, 1e-12)
    n=a.size
    N=next_fft_len(n)
    best=a.copy()
    c=convolve_fft(a,N)
    C=float(c.max())
    best_score=float(2.0*n*C)
    if not np.isfinite(best_score):
       return best, best_score
    step=0.7
    no_improve=0
    max_no_improve=350
    it=0
    print(f"start local n={n} score={best_score:.6f}")
    while time.monotonic() < deadline:
        it += 1
        c = convolve_fft(a,N)
        C = float(c.max())
        if not np.isfinite(C): break
        score = float(2.0*n*C)
        if score < best_score:
            best_score=score; best=a.copy(); no_improve=0
        if best_score <= 1e-12:
            break
        if best_score > 2.3:
            p=1.0
        else:
            p = 1.0 + max(0.0, 2.3 - best_score) * 20.0
            if p < 1.0: p=1.0
            if p > 80.0: p=80.0
        H = gradient_H(a,c,C,p,N)
        center = float(np.dot(a,H))
        delta = H - center
        span = float(delta.max() - delta.min())
        cand = None
        sc = float('inf')
        if span > 1e-14:
            cand = apply_log_shift(a, delta, step)
            if cand is None:
                cand = random_log_perturb(a, 0.05, rng)
            sc = fast_score_normalized(cand, N)
            if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < best_score - 1e-12:
                a = cand; best=a.copy(); best_score=sc; no_improve=0; step=min(2.0, step*1.02)
            else:
                cand2 = apply_log_shift(a, delta, step*0.5)
                if cand2 is not None:
                    sc2 = fast_score_normalized(cand2, N)
                    if np.isfinite(sc2) and sc2 < best_score - 1e-12:
                        a=cand2; best=a.copy(); best_score=sc2; no_improve=0; step=max(0.05, step*0.9)
                if best_score >= score: # no improve? Need track improved flag
                implement improved flag.
```

Implementation of `add_candidate`:
```
def add_candidate(top, seq, score, capacity=16):
    if seq is None or not np.isfinite(score):
        return
    arr = np.asarray(seq, dtype=np.float64).reshape(-1)
    if arr.size == 0:
        return
    arr = normalize_positive(arr)
    top.append((float(score), arr))
    top.sort(key=operator.itemgetter(0))
    if len(top) > capacity:
        del top[capacity:]
```
No lambda.

Potential memory: top arrays 16*8192 ~1MB. fine.

Now, initial top maybe many. Use capacity=10 to save. But we need top exact up to3. 10 fine.

```
def propose_candidate(seed=42, budget_s=400, **kwargs):
    start=time.monotonic()
    total_budget=float(budget_s)
    if total_budget <= 0: return [1.0]
    reserve=12.0 if total_budget<120 else 12.0
    search_deadline = start + max(1.0, total_budget - reserve)
    fourier_deadline = start + max(0.0, min(90.0, total_budget - 180.0)) # for 400 => start+90? total-180=220 min90 =>90. If total 60 => -120 =>0. Good.
    rng=np.random.default_rng(seed)
    top=[]
    parent = globals().get("height_sequence_1", None)
    if parent is not None:
       parr=np.asarray(parent, dtype=np.float64).reshape(-1)
       if parr.size >= 256 and parr.size <= 10000:
          pnorm=normalize_positive(parr, 1e-12)
          Np=next_fft_len(pnorm.size)
          sc=fast_score_normalized(pnorm,Np)
          add_candidate(top,pnorm,sc,12)
    lengths=[]
    if parent is not None:
       plen=int(np.asarray(parent,dtype=object).size?) # can't? use parr size global? better store parent_len.
```

```
def local_search(a, deadline, rng):
    a = normalize_positive(a, 1e-12)
    n = int(a.size)
    N = next_fft_len(n)
    L = 2*n - 1
    idx = (N - np.arange(n)) % N
    best = a.copy()
    fa = np.fft.rfft(a, N)
    c = np.fft.irfft(fa*fa, N)
    c = c[:L]
    c = np.where(c > 0.0, c, 0.0)
    C = float(c.max())
    best_score = float(2.0*n*C)
    if not np.isfinite(best_score):
        return best, best_score
    step = 0.7
    stall = 0
    perturb_attempts = 0
    max_no_improve = 350
    it = 0
    print("local_search n={} start_score={:.6f}".format(n, best_score), flush=True)
    while time.monotonic() < deadline:
        it += 1
        fa = np.fft.rfft(a, N)
        c = np.fft.irfft(fa*fa, N)
        c = c[:L]
        c = np.where(c > 0.0, c, 0.0)
        C = float(c.max())
        if not np.isfinite(C):
            break
        score = float(2.0*n*C)
        improved = False
        if score < best_score - 1e-12:
            best_score = score
            best = a.copy()
            stall = 0
        # dynamic p
        if best_score > 2.3:
            p = 1.0
        else:
            p = 1.0 + max(0.0, 2.3 - best_score) * 20.0
            if p < 1.0: p = 1.0
            if p > 80.0: p = 80.0
        H = gradient_H(a, c, C, p, N, fa, idx)
        center = float(np.dot(a, H))
        delta = H - center
        span = float(delta.max() - delta.min())
        if span > 1e-14:
            cand = apply_log_shift(a, delta, step)
            if cand is None:
                cand = random_log_perturb(a, 0.05, rng)
            sc = fast_score_normalized(cand, N)
            if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < best_score - 1e-12:
                a = cand
                best = a.copy()
                best_score = sc
                stall = 0
                improved = True
                step = min(2.0, step * 1.02)
            else:
                cand2 = apply_log_shift(a, delta, step * 0.5)
                if cand2 is not None:
                    sc2 = fast_score_normalized(cand2, N)
                    if np.isfinite(sc2) and sc2 < best_score - 1e-12:
                        a = cand2
                        best = a.copy()
                        best_score = sc2
                        stall = 0
                        improved = True
                        step = max(0.05, step * 0.9)
                if not improved:
                    stall += 1
                    step = max(0.02, step * 0.85)
        else:
            cand = random_log_perturb(a, 0.05, rng)
            sc = fast_score_normalized(cand, N)
            if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < best_score - 1e-12:
                a = cand; best = a.copy(); best_score = sc; stall=0; improved=True
            else:
                stall += 1
        if stall > 80:
            sigma = 0.03 + 0.07 * float(rng.random())
            cand = random_log_perturb(best, sigma, rng)
            sc = fast_score_normalized(cand, N)
            if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < best_score - 1e-12:
                a = cand; best = a.copy(); best_score = sc; step=0.7; stall=0
            else:
                a = best.copy()
                step = 0.7
                stall = 0
                perturb_attempts += 1
                if perturb_attempts > 14:
                    break
        if stall > max_no_improve:
            break
        if it % 1000 == 0:
            print("local n={} it={} score={:.6f} step={:.3f} p={:.1f} stall={}".format(n,it,best_score,step,p,stall), flush=True)
    return best, best_score
```
Note: If current `score` improved (because a changed? Shouldn't), we set best before candidate. Then candidate compares to updated best. okay.

One bug: If `span > 1e-14` but `apply_log_shift` returns candidate that is exact same (if delta zero? span zero no). Candidate fast score may equal best within tolerance, no improve. stall. okay.

```
def propose_candidate(seed=42, budget_s=400, **kwargs):
    start = time.monotonic()
    total_budget = max(1.0, float(budget_s))
    reserve = 12.0 if total_budget >= 60 else 4.0
    search_deadline = start + max(1.0, total_budget - reserve)
    fourier_deadline = start + max(0.0, min(90.0, total_budget - 180.0))
    rng = np.random.default_rng(seed)
    top = []
    parent_len = None
    parent = globals().get("height_sequence_1", None)
    if parent is not None:
        try:
            parr = np.asarray(parent, dtype=np.float64).reshape(-1)
            if parr.size >= 256 and parr.size <= 10000:
                parent_arr = normalize_positive(parr, 1e-12)
                parent_len = parent_arr.size
                Np = next_fft_len(parent_len)
                sc = fast_score_normalized(parent_arr, Np)
                add_candidate(top, parent_arr, sc, 16)
        except Exception:
            parent_arr = None
    lengths=[]
    for n in (4096, 8192):
       if n not in lengths: lengths.append(n)
    if parent_len is not None:
       lengths.append(parent_len)
    # Candidate generation
    for idx,n in enumerate(lengths):
       if time.monotonic() >= search_deadline - 5.0: break
       M = 6 if n >= 4096 else 4
       try:
          seqs = generate_basic_candidates(n, M, int(seed)+1000*(idx+1))
       except Exception:
          seqs=[np.ones(n)/n]
       N=next_fft_len(n)
       for seq in seqs:
          try:
             sc=fast_score_normalized(seq,N)
          except Exception:
             continue
          if np.isfinite(sc): add_candidate(top, seq, sc, 16)
       if time.monotonic() < fourier_deadline:
          if n <= 4096:
             fM=5; fmax=500
          elif n <= 8192:
             fM=4; fmax=300
          else:
             fM=3; fmax=200
          try:
             cands=fourier_search_candidates(n, fM, fmax, int(seed)+2000*(idx+1), fourier_deadline)
          except Exception:
             cands=[]
          for sc,seq in cands:
             add_candidate(top, seq, sc, 16)
       if top:
          print("init n={} best={:.6f} t={:.1f}".format(n, top[0][0], time.monotonic()-start), flush=True)
    if not top:
       seq=np.ones(1024,dtype=float)/1024
       sc=fast_score_normalized(seq)
       top=[(sc,seq)]
    top.sort(key=operator.itemgetter(0))
    # Local search
    local_end = search_deadline
    if time.monotonic() < local_end:
       fracs=(0.55,0.35,0.10)
       upto = min(3, len(top))
       for i in range(upto):
          if time.monotonic() >= local_end: break
          rem = local_end - time.monotonic()
          if rem < 5.0: break
          frac = fracs[i] if i < len(fracs) else 0.0
          alloc = rem * frac
          if rem > 60.0 and alloc < 10.0:
              alloc = 10.0
          end = time.monotonic() + min(alloc, rem)
          seq, sc = local_search(top[i][1], end, rng)
          if np.isfinite(sc):
              add_candidate(top, seq, sc, 16)
          top.sort(key=operator.itemgetter(0))
          print("local done i={} best={:.6f} t={:.1f}".format(i, top[0][0] if top else float('inf'), time.monotonic()-start), flush=True)
    if top:
       top.sort(key=operator.itemgetter(0))
    # exact eval
    exact_seq=None; exact_score=float('inf')
    final_hard_deadline=start + total_budget - 0.5
    for i in range(min(3,len(top))):
       if time.monotonic() > final_hard_deadline - 1.0: break
       seq=top[i][1]
       if seq.size > 10000: continue
       try:
           s=float(evaluate_sequence(seq.tolist()))
       except Exception:
           s=float('inf')
       if np.isfinite(s) and s < exact_score:
           exact_score=s; exact_seq=seq.copy()
       print("exact candidate {} score={:.6f}".format(i,s), flush=True)
       if time.monotonic() > final_hard_deadline - 1.0: break
    if exact_seq is not None:
       final=exact_seq
    elif top:
       final=top[0][1]
    else:
       final=np.ones(1)
    final=np.maximum(np.asarray(final,dtype=float),0.0)
    return [float(x) for x in final]
```

Now, let's reason through the `r` correction one more time with a concrete n=3 to ensure H orientation.

```python
"""
Search for nonnegative sequences minimizing
    2 * n * max(convolve(a, a)) / (sum(a)^2).

The program combines:
- Fourier-style smooth initializations,
- Nelder-Mead optimization over a small number of Fourier coefficients,
- exponentiated-gradient / power-weighted local search on the full sequence,
- a final exact validation step using evaluate_sequence.

The method is self-contained and time-boxed.  The returned sequence is the
best validated sequence found before the deadline.
"""

import numpy as np
import time
import operator

try:
    from scipy.optimize import minimize
    _HAVE_SCIPY = True
except Exception:
    _HAVE_SCIPY = False


def next_fft_len(n):
    """Smallest power of two length large enough for full convolution."""
    L = 2 * int(n) - 1
    N = 1
    while N < L:
        N <<= 1
    return int(N)


def normalize_positive(a, floor=0.0):
    """Clip to nonnegative values and normalize sum to one."""
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).reshape(-1)
    if a.size == 0:
        return np.ones(1, dtype=np.float64)

    a = np.where(np.isfinite(a), a, 0.0)
    a = np.maximum(a, 0.0)

    s = float(a.sum())
    if not np.isfinite(s) or s <= 1e-300:
        a = np.ones_like(a)
        s = float(a.sum())

    a = a / s

    if floor > 0.0:
        a = np.maximum(a, float(floor))
        s = float(a.sum())
        if np.isfinite(s) and s > 0.0:
            a = a / s

    return a


def convolve_fft(a, N=None):
    """Full linear convolution a*a using FFT, nonnegatively clipped."""
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64)
    n = a.size
    L = 2 * n - 1

    if N is None:
        N = next_fft_len(n)
    else:
        N = int(N)
        if N < L:
            N = next_fft_len(n)

    fa = np.fft.rfft(a, N)
    c = np.fft.irfft(fa * fa, N)
    c = c[:L]
    c = np.where(c > 0.0, c, 0.0)
    return c


def fast_score_normalized(a, N=None):
    """Fast FFT-based objective for a normalized nonnegative array."""
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64)
    n = a.size
    if n == 0:
        return float("inf")

    s = float(a.sum())
    if not np.isfinite(s) or s <= 1e-300:
        return float("inf")

    if abs(s - 1.0) > 1e-9:
        a = a / s

    c = convolve_fft(a, N)
    C = float(c.max()) if c.size else 0.0
    if not np.isfinite(C):
        return float("inf")

    return float(2.0 * n * C)


def basis_for_fourier(n, M):
    """Precompute sine/cosine tables for a low-dimensional Fourier shape."""
    n = int(n)
    M = int(M)

    if n <= 4:
        M = 1
    else:
        M = max(1, min(M, n // 4))

    if n == 1:
        return np.zeros((M, 1)), np.ones((M, 1))

    x = (np.arange(n, dtype=np.float64) + 0.5) / float(n)
    k = (2.0 * np.pi) * np.arange(1, M + 1, dtype=np.float64)[:, None]
    angles = k * x[None, :]

    sin_tab = np.sin(angles)
    cos_tab = np.cos(angles)
    return sin_tab, cos_tab


def make_fourier_basis(coeffs, sin_tab, cos_tab):
    """Construct a nonnegative normalized sequence from Fourier coefficients."""
    M = sin_tab.shape[0]
    n = sin_tab.shape[1]

    coeffs = np.asarray(coeffs, dtype=np.float64).reshape(-1)
    if coeffs.size < 2 * M:
        tmp = np.zeros(2 * M, dtype=np.float64)
        tmp[:coeffs.size] = coeffs
        coeffs = tmp

    coeffs = coeffs[:2 * M]
    coeffs = np.where(np.isfinite(coeffs), coeffs, 0.0)
    coeffs = np.clip(coeffs, -1.0, 1.0)

    f = 1.0 + coeffs[0::2] @ sin_tab + coeffs[1::2] @ cos_tab
    f = np.maximum(f, 0.0)
    f = f + 1e-10

    s = float(f.sum())
    if not np.isfinite(s) or s <= 0.0:
        f = np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64)
        s = float(f.sum())

    f = f / s
    return f


def fourier_objective(coeffs, sin_tab, cos_tab, N):
    """Objective for low-dimensional Fourier optimization."""
    a = make_fourier_basis(coeffs, sin_tab, cos_tab)
    return fast_score_normalized(a, N)


def random_positive_candidates(n, count, seed):
    """A few random positive full-support candidates."""
    n = int(n)
    count = int(count)
    rng = np.random.default_rng(seed)
    out = []

    for i in range(count):
        kind = i % 3
        if kind == 0:
            a = np.exp(rng.normal(0.0, 0.35, size=n))
        elif kind == 1:
            a = rng.gamma(2.0, size=n)
        else:
            a = np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64) + 0.6 * rng.normal(0.0, 1.0, size=n)

        a = normalize_positive(a, 1e-12)
        out.append(a)

    return out


def generate_basic_candidates(n, M, seed):
    """Basic candidate generators for a fixed length."""
    n = int(n)
    M = max(1, min(M, n // 4 if n > 4 else 1))
    sin_tab, cos_tab = basis_for_fourier(n, M)
    rng = np.random.default_rng(seed)
    out = []

    # Uniform baseline.
    out.append(np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64) / float(n))

    # Single sine modulation around 1.
    for al in (0.3, 0.5, 0.7, 0.9, -0.3, -0.5, -0.7):
        coeffs = np.zeros(2 * M, dtype=np.float64)
        coeffs[0] = al
        out.append(make_fourier_basis(coeffs, sin_tab, cos_tab))

    # Two-mode useful starts.
    if M >= 2:
        coeffs = np.zeros(2 * M, dtype=np.float64)
        coeffs[0] = 0.5
        coeffs[2] = 0.3
        out.append(make_fourier_basis(coeffs, sin_tab, cos_tab))

        coeffs = np.zeros(2 * M, dtype=np.float64)
        coeffs[0] = 0.5
        coeffs[2] = -0.3
        out.append(make_fourier_basis(coeffs, sin_tab, cos_tab))

    # Random smooth Fourier starts.
    for _ in range(8):
        coeffs = rng.normal(0.0, 0.35, size=2 * M)
        if coeffs.size > 0:
            coeffs[0] += 0.4 * (1.0 if rng.random() < 0.5 else -1.0)
        out.append(make_fourier_basis(coeffs, sin_tab, cos_tab))

    # Random positive full-support starts.
    out.extend(random_positive_candidates(n, 3, seed + 12345))
    return out


def fourier_search_candidates(n, M, maxiter, seed, limit_deadline):
    """Small-dimensional Fourier optimization plus random fallback."""
    n = int(n)
    M = max(1, min(M, n // 64 if n > 64 else 1))
    sin_tab, cos_tab = basis_for_fourier(n, M)
    N = next_fft_len(n)
    rng = np.random.default_rng(seed)

    starts = []

    c = np.zeros(2 * M, dtype=np.float64)
    c[0] = 0.5
    starts.append(c.copy())

    c = np.zeros(2 * M, dtype=np.float64)
    c[0] = -0.5
    starts.append(c.copy())

    c = np.zeros(2 * M, dtype=np.float64)
    c[0] = 0.5
    if M >= 2:
        c[2] = 0.3
    starts.append(c.copy())

    c = np.zeros(2 * M, dtype=np.float64)
    c[0] = 0.5
    if M >= 2:
        c[2] = -0.3
    starts.append(c.copy())

    for _ in range(4):
        starts.append(rng.normal(0.0, 0.4, size=2 * M))

    found = []

    if _HAVE_SCIPY:
        for x0 in starts:
            if time.monotonic() >= limit_deadline:
                break
            try:
                res = minimize(
                    fourier_objective,
                    x0,
                    args=(sin_tab, cos_tab, N),
                    method="Nelder-Mead",
                    options={
                        "maxiter": int(maxiter),
                        "xatol": 1e-5,
                        "fatol": 1e-5,
                    },
                )
                if res.fun is not None and np.isfinite(res.fun):
                    a = make_fourier_basis(res.x, sin_tab, cos_tab)
                    sc = fast_score_normalized(a, N)
                    if np.isfinite(sc):
                        found.append((float(sc), a.copy()))
            except Exception:
                pass

    count = 30 if not _HAVE_SCIPY else 8
    for i in range(count):
        x0 = rng.normal(0.0, 0.4, size=2 * M)
        if x0.size > 0:
            x0[0] = 0.4 * (1.0 if rng.random() < 0.5 else -1.0) + 0.1 * float(rng.normal())
        a = make_fourier_basis(x0, sin_tab, cos_tab)
        sc = fast_score_normalized(a, N)
        if np.isfinite(sc):
            found.append((float(sc), a.copy()))

    found.sort(key=operator.itemgetter(0))

    res = []
    for sc, a in found[:12]:
        if np.isfinite(sc):
            res.append((float(sc), a.copy()))
            if len(res) >= 6:
                break

    return res


def gradient_H(a, c, C, p, N, fa=None, idx=None):
    """
    Gradient vector proportional to H_i = sum_k w_k a_{k-i},
    where w_k are power weights based on convolution peaks.
    """
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64)
    n = a.size
    L = 2 * n - 1

    C = float(C)
    if not np.isfinite(C) or C <= 0.0:
        return np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)

    if N is None:
        N = next_fft_len(n)
    else:
        N = int(N)
        if N < L:
            N = next_fft_len(n)

    c = np.asarray(c, dtype=np.float64).reshape(-1)
    if c.size != L:
        tmp = np.zeros(L, dtype=np.float64)
        m = min(L, c.size)
        tmp[:m] = c[:m]
        c = tmp

    x = c / C
    x = np.clip(x, 0.0, 1.0)

    p = float(p)
    if p <= 1e-12:
        w = np.ones(L, dtype=np.float64)
    else:
        with np.errstate(over="ignore", under="ignore", invalid="ignore"):
            w = np.power(x, p)
        w = np.where(np.isfinite(w), w, 0.0)

    s = float(w.sum())
    if not np.isfinite(s) or s <= 1e-300:
        w = c.copy()
        s = float(w.sum())
    if not np.isfinite(s) or s <= 1e-300:
        w = np.ones(L, dtype=np.float64)
        s = float(w.sum())

    w = w / s

    r = np.zeros(N, dtype=np.float64)
    r[0] = w[0]
    if L > 1:
        # r[N-k] = w[k], for k = 1..L-1.
        stop = N - L
        if stop < 0:
            stop = 0
        r[N - 1:stop:-1] = w[1:]

    if fa is None:
        fa = np.fft.rfft(a, N)

    fr = np.fft.rfft(r, N)
    h = np.fft.irfft(fa * fr, N)

    if idx is None:
        idx = (N - np.arange(n)) % N

    H = h[idx]
    H = np.where(H > 0.0, H, 0.0)

    if float(H.max()) <= 0.0:
        H = np.ones_like(a)
        H /= float(H.sum())

    return H


def apply_log_shift(a, delta, step):
    """Multiplicative update a_i *= exp(-lambda * delta_i), then renormalize."""
    delta = np.asarray(delta, dtype=np.float64)
    if delta.size != a.size:
        return None

    span = float(delta.max() - delta.min())
    if not np.isfinite(span) or span <= 1e-15:
        return None

    lam = float(step) / span
    if not np.isfinite(lam):
        return None

    ex = -lam * delta
    ex = np.clip(ex, -8.0, 8.0)

    cand = a * np.exp(ex)
    cand = np.maximum(cand, 1e-12)

    s = float(cand.sum())
    if not np.isfinite(s) or s <= 0.0:
        return None

    cand = cand / s
    return cand


def random_log_perturb(a, sigma, rng):
    """Apply a multiplicative random perturbation in log-space."""
    base = np.maximum(a, 1e-12)
    loga = np.log(base)
    loga = loga + rng.normal(0.0, float(sigma), size=a.size)
    loga = np.clip(loga, -60.0, 60.0)

    cand = np.exp(loga)
    cand = np.maximum(cand, 1e-12)
    s = float(cand.sum())

    if not np.isfinite(s) or s <= 0.0:
        return base.copy()

    return cand / s


def local_search(a, deadline, rng):
    """
    Full-length exponentiated-gradient search.

    The weights on convolution peaks are c_k^p, with p increasing as the
    current objective improves.  Steps are normalized by the range of the
    gradient and accepted only when the fast FFT objective decreases.
    """
    a = normalize_positive(a, 1e-12)
    n = int(a.size)
    N = next_fft_len(n)
    L = 2 * n - 1
    idx = (N - np.arange(n)) % N

    best = a.copy()

    fa = np.fft.rfft(a, N)
    c = np.fft.irfft(fa * fa, N)
    c = c[:L]
    c = np.where(c > 0.0, c, 0.0)
    C = float(c.max())
    best_score = float(2.0 * n * C)

    if not np.isfinite(best_score):
        return best, best_score

    step = 0.7
    stall = 0
    perturb_attempts = 0
    max_no_improve = 400
    it = 0

    print("local_search n={} start_score={:.6f}".format(n, best_score), flush=True)

    while time.monotonic() < deadline:
        it += 1

        fa = np.fft.rfft(a, N)
        c = np.fft.irfft(fa * fa, N)
        c = c[:L]
        c = np.where(c > 0.0, c, 0.0)

        C = float(c.max())
        if not np.isfinite(C) or C <= 0.0:
            break

        score = float(2.0 * n * C)
        improved = False

        if score < best_score - 1e-12:
            best_score = score
            best = a.copy()
            stall = 0

        if best_score > 2.3:
            p = 1.0
        else:
            p = 1.0 + max(0.0, 2.3 - best_score) * 20.0
            p = min(80.0, max(1.0, p))

        H = gradient_H(a, c, C, p, N, fa, idx)
        center = float(np.dot(a, H))
        delta = H - center
        span = float(delta.max() - delta.min())

        if span > 1e-14:
            cand = apply_log_shift(a, delta, step)
            if cand is None:
                cand = random_log_perturb(a, 0.05, rng)

            sc = fast_score_normalized(cand, N)
            if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < best_score - 1e-12:
                a = cand
                best = a.copy()
                best_score = sc
                stall = 0
                improved = True
                step = min(2.0, step * 1.02)
            else:
                cand2 = apply_log_shift(a, delta, step * 0.5)
                if cand2 is not None:
                    sc2 = fast_score_normalized(cand2, N)
                    if np.isfinite(sc2) and sc2 < best_score - 1e-12:
                        a = cand2
                        best = a.copy()
                        best_score = sc2
                        stall = 0
                        improved = True
                        step = max(0.05, step * 0.9)

                if not improved:
                    stall += 1
                    step = max(0.02, step * 0.85)
        else:
            cand = random_log_perturb(a, 0.05, rng)
            sc = fast_score_normalized(cand, N)
            if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < best_score - 1e-12:
                a = cand
                best = a.copy()
                best_score = sc
                stall = 0
                improved = True
            else:
                stall += 1

        if stall > 80:
            sigma = 0.03 + 0.07 * float(rng.random())
            cand = random_log_perturb(best, sigma, rng)
            sc = fast_score_normalized(cand, N)
            if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < best_score - 1e-12:
                a = cand
                best = a.copy()
                best_score = sc
                step = 0.7
                stall = 0
            else:
                a = best.copy()
                step = 0.7
                stall = 0
                perturb_attempts += 1
                if perturb_attempts > 14:
                    break

        if stall > max_no_improve:
            break

        if it % 1000 == 0:
            print(
                "local n={} it={} score={:.6f} step={:.3f} p={:.1f} stall={}".format(
                    n, it, best_score, step, p, stall
                ),
                flush=True,
            )

    return best, best_score


def add_candidate(top, seq, score, capacity=20):
    """Maintain a small top list of (score, array) candidates."""
    if seq is None:
        return
    if score is None or not np.isfinite(score):
        return

    arr = np.asarray(seq, dtype=np.float64).reshape(-1)
    if arr.size == 0:
        return

    arr = normalize_positive(arr)
    top.append((float(score), arr))
    top.sort(key=operator.itemgetter(0))

    if len(top) > capacity:
        del top[capacity:]


def propose_candidate(seed=42, budget_s=400, **kwargs):
    """
    Time-boxed search entry point.

    Returns a list of nonnegative floats.
    """
    start = time.monotonic()
    total_budget = max(1.0, float(budget_s))

    reserve = 12.0 if total_budget >= 60.0 else 4.0
    search_deadline = start + max(1.0, total_budget - reserve)

    # Reserve a modest part of the run for Fourier init if time allows.
    fourier_deadline = start + max(0.0, min(90.0, total_budget - 180.0))

    rng = np.random.default_rng(seed)
    top = []

    parent_arr = None
    parent_score = float("inf")
    parent = globals().get("height_sequence_1", None)

    if parent is not None:
        try:
            parr = np.asarray(parent, dtype=np.float64).reshape(-1)
            if parr.size >= 256 and parr.size <= 10000:
                parent_arr = normalize_positive(parr, 1e-12)
                Np = next_fft_len(parent_arr.size)
                parent_score = fast_score_normalized(parent_arr, Np)
                add_candidate(top, parent_arr, parent_score, 20)
        except Exception:
            parent_arr = None

    lengths = []
    for n in (4096, 8192):
        if n not in lengths:
            lengths.append(n)
    if parent_arr is not None:
        if parent_arr.size not in lengths:
            lengths.append(parent_arr.size)

    # Generate initial candidates.
    for idx, n in enumerate(lengths):
        if time.monotonic() >= search_deadline - 5.0:
            break

        M = 6 if n >= 4096 else 4
        seed_n = int(seed) + 1000 * (idx + 1)

        try:
            seqs = generate_basic_candidates(n, M, seed_n)
        except Exception:
            seqs = [np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64) / float(n)]

        N = next_fft_len(n)

        for seq in seqs:
            try:
                sc = fast_score_normalized(seq, N)
            except Exception:
                sc = float("inf")
            if np.isfinite(sc):
                add_candidate(top, seq, sc, 20)

        if time.monotonic() < fourier_deadline:
            if n <= 4096:
                fM = 5
                fmax = 400
            elif n <= 8192:
                fM = 4
                fmax = 250
            else:
                fM = 3
                fmax = 150

            try:
                cands = fourier_search_candidates(
                    n,
                    fM,
                    fmax,
                    int(seed) + 2000 * (idx + 1),
                    fourier_deadline,
                )
            except Exception:
                cands = []

            for sc, seq in cands:
                add_candidate(top, seq, sc, 20)

        if top:
            print(
                "init n={} best={} t={:.1f}".format(
                    n, top[0][0], time.monotonic() - start
                ),
                flush=True,
            )

    if not top:
        seq = np.ones(1024, dtype=np.float64) / 1024.0
        sc = fast_score_normalized(seq)
        top = [(float(sc), seq.copy())]

    top.sort(key=operator.itemgetter(0))

    # Full-length local searches.
    local_pool = []
    for item in top[:3]:
        local_pool.append(item)

    if parent_arr is not None and np.isfinite(parent_score):
        local_pool.append((float(parent_score), parent_arr.copy()))

    local_pool.sort(key=operator.itemgetter(0))
    local_pool = local_pool[:4]

    local_end = search_deadline
    fracs = (0.55, 0.35, 0.10)

    for i in range(min(3, len(local_pool))):
        now = time.monotonic()
        if now >= local_end:
            break

        rem = local_end - now
        if rem < 5.0:
            break

        frac = fracs[i] if i < len(fracs) else 0.0
        alloc = rem * frac
        if rem > 60.0 and alloc < 10.0:
            alloc = 10.0

        end = now + min(alloc, rem)
        sc, seq = local_local = None, None
        try:
            seq2, sc = local_search(local_pool[i][1], end, rng)
        except Exception:
            seq2, sc = local_pool[i][1], local_pool[i][0]

        if np.isfinite(sc):
            add_candidate(top, seq2, sc, 20)

        top.sort(key=operator.itemgetter(0))
        print(
            "local done i={} best={} t={:.1f}".format(
                i, top[0][0] if top else float("inf"), time.monotonic() - start
            ),
            flush=True,
        )

    if top:
        top.sort(key=operator.itemgetter(0))

    # Exact validation of a few final candidates.
    exact_seq = None
    exact_score = float("inf")
    final_hard_deadline = start + total_budget - 0.5
    exact_margin = 6.0

    for i in range(min(3, len(top))):
        if time.monotonic() + exact_margin > final_hard_deadline:
            break

        seq = top[i][1]
        if seq.size > 10000:
            continue

        try:
            s = float(evaluate_sequence(seq.tolist()))
        except Exception:
            s = float("inf")

        if np.isfinite(s) and s < exact_score:
            exact_score = s
            exact_seq = seq.copy()

        print("exact candidate {} score={:.6f}".format(i, s), flush=True)

    if exact_seq is not None:
        final = exact_seq
    elif top:
        final = top[0][1]
    else:
        final = np.ones(1, dtype=np.float64)

    final = np.asarray(final, dtype=np.float64)
    final = np.maximum(final, 0.0)

    return [float(x) for x in final]
```
